<script type="text/javascript">
  jQuery(function() {
    "use strict";

    var signupForm = jQuery("#signup-popover-frel_overlay .signup-popover-popover__form");

    signupForm.submit(submitHandler);

    function submitHandler(e) {
      e.preventDefault();

      var formTickbox = $('input[type="checkbox"]').is(":checked"),
      contentMessage = jQuery(
        "#signup-popover-frel_overlay .signup-popover-popover__text--error"
      );

      if (formTickbox) {
        frelCloseModal();
        jQuery("#newsletterModal hr, #newsletterModal div.buttons").hide();
        jQuery("#newsletterModal").modal("show");

      } else {
        var formInvalid = 'You must agree to the Terms & Conditions';
        contentMessage.text(formInvalid);
      }
    }

    var root = document.querySelector(".frel_overlay");

    root.addEventListener("click", modalClick);
    root.addEventListener("touchstart", modalClick);
    document.addEventListener("keyup", modalKeyup);

    function modalClick(e) {
      var targetClasses = e.target.classList;
      if (
        targetClasses.contains("signup-popover-container") ||
        targetClasses.contains("signup-popover-popover__button--close")
      ) {
        frelDismissModal();
      }
    }

    function modalKeyup(e) {
      if (e.keyCode === 27) {
        frelDismissModal();
      }
    }

    function frelCloseModal() {
      root.classList.add("signup-popover-popover--hide");
    }

    function frelDismissModal() {
      frelCloseModal();
      $TB.popoverDismissed("signup-popover");
    }
  });
</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
  var popup;
  var ouibounceCallback = function () {
      $TB.popoverShown('signup-popover');
  };
  try {
      jQuery.getScript("//cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/ouibounce/0.0.11/ouibounce.min.js", function (data, textStatus, jqxhr) {
          popup = ouibounce(document.getElementById("signup-popover-frel_overlay"), {
              cookieExpire: 365,
              cookieName: "newsPopup",
              callback: ouibounceCallback,
              sitewide: true,
              aggressive: false,
              timer: 38000,
              
              delay: 250,
              
          });
          
          window.setTimeout(function () {
              if (document.cookie.replace(/(?:(?:^|.*;\s*)newsPopup\s*\=\s*([^;]*).*$)|^.*$/, "$1") !== "true" || false) {
                  popup.fire();
                  $TB.popoverShown('signup-popover');
              }
          }, 38000);
          
      });
  } catch(e) {}
</script></div>

<script>
    function quickViewFunction(event, ProductCode, openSize, openWishlist) {
        ShowLoading($('#product-quick-view'));
        var quickView = '';
        $('#quickViewPanel').data('mmenu').open();

        var model = { productCode: ProductCode };
        $.ajax({
            type: 'GET',
            url: '/umbraco/Surface/ProductSurface/GetQuickView/',
            dataType: 'json',
            data: model,
            cache: false,
            success: function (d) {
                //console.log(d);

                if (d != undefined && d !== "") {
                    var item = d.product;
                    //console.log(item);

                    var name = item.ProductName.replace(/[^\w]/gi, '-').toLowerCase();
                    var productUrl = /product-detail/ + name + "/" + item.ProductCode;

                    // Get product type
                    var isBookOrVinyl = d.productType.SystemCode == "BOOK" || d.productType.SystemCode == "VINYL";
                    var isMerchandise = d.productType.SystemCode == "MERCH";

                    // Get product image
                    var productImage = "/Assets/Images/placeholder.png";
                    if (item.ProductImage.length) {
                        productImage = 'https://images.arrowfilms.com' + item.ProductImage
                    }

                    if (item.ImageText != null && item.ImageText.trim() !== "") {
                        quickView += '<div id="product-image-text">' + item.ImageText + '</div>';
                    }

                    quickView += '<div class="closeIcon pull-right"></div>' +
                    '<div class="row">' +
                    '<div class="col-lg-6 col-sm-6 col-xs-12">' +
                    '<div id="productImage">' +
                    '<img src="' + productImage + '?width=446" alt="' + item.ProductName + '" />' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '<div class="col-lg-6 col-sm-6 col-xs-12 mt-20-mobile">' +
                    '<div class="product-name">' + item.ProductName + '</div>';

                    if (!isBookOrVinyl && !isMerchandise) {
                        quickView += '<div class="genre">' +
                            d.productGenre;
                        if (d.productSubGenre) { quickView += ' / ' + d.productSubGenre; }
                        if (d.productSubSubGenre) { quickView += ' / ' + d.productSubSubGenre; }
                        quickView += '</div>';
                    }
                    else if (isBookOrVinyl) {
                        quickView += '<div class="genre">By ' + item.Author + '</div>';
                    }
                    else if (isMerchandise) {
                        quickView += '<div class="genre">' + d.merchandiseTypeName + '</div>';
                    }

                    quickView += '<hr />' +
                    '<div class="product-specifications">';

                    if (isBookOrVinyl) {
                        quickView += '<div class="row">' +
                        '<div class="form-group">' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-4 col-sm-12 col-xs-12">' +
                        'Number of Pages' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-8 col-sm-12 col-xs-12" id="product-code">' +
                        item.NumberOfPages +
                        '</label>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>';
                    }

                    quickView += '<div class="row">' +
                    '<div class="form-group">' +
                    '<label class="control-label col-md-4 col-sm-12 col-xs-12">' +
                    'Product Code' +
                    '</label>' +
                    '<label class="control-label col-md-8 col-sm-12 col-xs-12" id="product-code">' +
                    item.ProductCode +
                    '</label>' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '</div>';

                    if (!item.ArrowVideoUrl) {
                        var inStockText = '';
                        var inStockClass = '';

                        if (new Date(item.ReleaseDate) > new Date() && item.CurrentStockLevel > 0) {
                            inStockText = 'Pre-Order';
                            inStockClass = 'stock-availability-green';
                        }
                        else if (item.AmberStockLevelLimit < item.CurrentStockLevel) {
                            inStockText = 'In Stock';
                            inStockClass = 'stock-availability-green';
                        }
                        else if ((item.CurrentStockLevel <= item.AmberStockLevelLimit && item.CurrentStockLevel > item.RedStockLevelLimit) || item.CurrentStockLevel < 20 && item.CurrentStockLevel > 0) {
                            inStockText = 'Limited Availability';
                            inStockClass = 'stock-availability-amber';
                        }
                        else if (item.CurrentStockLevel < item.RedStockLevelLimit && item.CurrentStockLevel > 0) {
                            inStockText = 'Last Few';
                            inStockClass = 'stock-availability-red';
                        }
                        else if (item.CurrentStockLevel == 0) {
                            inStockText = item.OutOfStockText;
                            inStockClass = 'stock-availability-red';
                        }

                        quickView += '<div class="row">' +
                        '<div class="form-group" id="availability-details">' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-4 col-sm-12 col-xs-12">' +
                        'Availability' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-8 col-sm-12 col-xs-12">' +
                        '<span class="stock-availability' + inStockClass + '">' +
                        '<span>' + inStockText + '</span>' +
                        '</span>' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>';
                    }

                    if (item.ReleaseDate) {
                        quickView += '<div class="row">' +
                        '<div class="form-group" id="product-release-date-details">' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-4 col-sm-12 col-xs-12">' +
                        'Release Date' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-8 col-sm-12 col-xs-12">' +
                        '<span id="product-release-date">' +
                        moment(item.ReleaseDate).format('DD/MM/YYYY') +
                        '</span>' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>';
                    }

                    if ((!item.ArrowVideoUrl && isBookOrVinyl) || (!item.ArrowVideoUrl) && isMerchandise) {
                        quickView += '<hr class="mt-5" />';
                    }

                    if (isMerchandise) {
                        quickView += '<div class="row">' +
                        '<div class="form-group" id="product-release-date-details">' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-4 col-sm-12 col-xs-12">' +
                        'Size' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '<div class="control-label col-md-8 col-sm-12 col-xs-12">' +
                        '<div class="select-wrapper">' +
                        '<select class="form-control" id="ProductSizeChange" onchange="QuickViewChangeSize(this)">';

                        for (var x = 0; x < d.productSizes.length; x++) {
                            var selected = "";
                            if (d.productSizes[x].OtherProductSizeId == item.SizeId) {
                                selected = "selected ";
                            }

                            quickView += '<option ' +
                            selected +
                            'value="' +
                            d.productSizes[x].OtherProductId +
                            '">' + d.productSizes[x].OtherProductSizeName + '</option>';
                        }

                        quickView += '</select>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>';
                    }

                    if (item.CurrentStockLevel > 0 && !item.ArrowVideoUrl) {
                        var maxQty = item.MaxQuantityPerTransaction > 0 ? item.MaxQuantityPerTransaction : item.CurrentStockLevel;

                        quickView += '<div class="row">' +
                        '<div class="form-group" id="product-quantity-details">' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-4 col-sm-12 col-xs-12">' +
                        'Quantity' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '<div class="col-md-8 col-sm-12 col-xs-12 quantity-selector">' +
                        '<div class="input-group">' +
                        '<span class="input-group-btn">' +
                        '<button type="button" class="btn btn-default btn-number" data-type="minus" data-field="quant[2]">' +
                        '<i class="fa fa-minus"></i>' +
                        '</button>' +
                        '</span>' +
                        '<input type="text" name="quant[2]" id="basket-quantity" class="form-control input-number product-quick-view-quantity" value="1" min="1" max="' + maxQty + '">' +
                        '<span class="input-group-btn">' +
                        '<button type="button" class="btn btn-default btn-number" data-type="plus" data-field="quant[2]">' +
                        '<i class="fa fa-plus"></i>' +
                        '</button>' +
                        '</span>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>';
                    }

                    quickView += '<hr class="mt-5">' +
                    '<div class="row">' +
                    '<div class="form-group">' +
                    '<label class="control-label col-md-4 col-sm-12 col-xs-12 reward-text">' +
                    'Reward Points Rewarded' +
                    '</label>' +
                    '<label class="control-label col-md-8 col-sm-12 col-xs-12">' +
                    '<span id="reward-points-rewarded">' +
                    item.RewardPointsGiven +
                    '</span>' +
                    '</label>' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '<div class="row">' +
                    '<div class="form-group">' +
                    '<label class="control-label col-md-4 col-sm-12 col-xs-12 reward-text">' +
                    'Reward Points Needed' +
                    '</label>' +
                    '<label class="control-label col-md-8 col-sm-12 col-xs-12">' +
                    '<span id="reward-points-needed">' +
                    item.RewardPointsToBuy +
                    '</span>' +
                    '</label>' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '</div>';

                    if (item.RetailPrice > 0) {
                        quickView += '<div class="row">' +
                        '<div class="form-group rrp">' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-4 col-sm-12 col-xs-12 strike">' +
                        'RRP' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-8 col-sm-12 col-xs-12 strike">' +
                        '&pound;<span id="rrp-price">' +
                        item.RetailPrice.toFixed(2) +
                        '</span>' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>';
                    }

                    var productPrice = item.StandardPrice;
                    if (item.OfferPrice > 0.00) {
                        productPrice = item.OfferPrice;
                        quickView += '<div class="row">' +
                        '<div class="form-group ">' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-4 col-sm-12 col-xs-12 strike">' +
                        'Price' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-8 col-sm-12 col-xs-12 strike">' +
                        '&pound;<span id="price">' +
                        item.StandardPrice.toFixed(2) +
                        '</span>' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '<div class="row">' +
                        '<div class="form-group ">' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-4 col-sm-12 col-xs-12 gold-text">' +
                        'SALE' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-8 col-sm-12 col-xs-12 gold-text">' +
                        '&pound;<span id="sales-price">' +
                        item.OfferPrice.toFixed(2) +
                        '</span>' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>';
                    } else {
                        quickView += '<div class="row">' +
                        '<div class="form-group ">' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-4 col-sm-12 col-xs-12 gold-text">' +
                        'Price' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '<label class="control-label col-md-8 col-sm-12 col-xs-12 gold-text">' +
                        '&pound;<span id="price">' +
                        item.StandardPrice.toFixed(2) +
                        '</span>' +
                        '</label>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>';
                    }

                    quickView += '<hr class="mt-5">' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '<div class="row" id="product-action-buttons">';

                    // Add to basket
                    if (item.CurrentStockLevel > 0 && !item.ArrowVideoUrl) {
                        if (false && !item.ArrowVideoUrl) {
                            quickView += '<div class="col-sm-12 mb-20" id="AddToBasketSection">';
                        }
                        else {
                            quickView += '<div class="col-sm-6" id="AddToBasketSection">';
                        }
                        quickView += '<a class="btn gold-btn btn-lg" onclick="AddProductToCart(' + item.ProductId + ', ' + item.productName + ', ' + item.productCode + ', ' + productPrice + ', ' + item.productTypeName + ')">' +
                        '<span class="icon-basket"></span>' +
                        'ADD TO CART' +
                        '</a>' +
                        '</div>';
                    }

                    // Add to wishlist
                    if (false && !item.ArrowVideoUrl) {
                        quickView += '<div class="col-sm-6">' +
                        '<button type="button" id="add-wish-list-btn" class="btn btn-default btn-lg dropdown-toggle" data-toggle="dropdown" aria-haspopup="true" aria-expanded="false">' +
                        '<span class="icon-wishlist"></span>' +
                        'Add To WishList' +
                        '</button>' +
                        '<ul class="dropdown-menu" id="wish-list-items"></ul>' +
                        '</div>';
                    }

                    // Product detail button
                    if (!item.ArrowVideoUrl) {
                        quickView += '<div class="col-sm-6">' +
                            '<a class="btn btn-default light-grey-btn btn-lg" id="ProductDetailPageButton" href="' +
                            productUrl +
                            '">' +
                            '<div class="crossIcon"></div>' +
                            'PRODUCT DETAILS' +
                            '</a>' +
                            '</div>';
                    }

                    quickView += '</div>';

                    // Buy now button
                    if (item.ArrowVideoUrl) {
                        quickView += '<div class="row" id="product-video-button">' +
                        '<div class="col-sm-12">' +
                        'Product Detail' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '<div class="col-sm-6 mt-15">' +
                        '<a href="' +
                        item.ArrowVideoUrl +
                        '" class="btn gold-btn btn-lg" type="button" id="VideoBuyNowButton" target="_blank">' +
                        'Buy Now' +
                        '</a>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '</div>';
                    }

                    quickView += '</div>' +
                    '</div>' +
                    '</div>';

                    $('#product-quick-view').html(quickView);

                    QuantitySelector();

                    if (false && true) {
                        GetWishList(item.ProductId);
                    }

                    $("#quickViewPanel .closeIcon").on('click', function () {
                        $('#quickViewPanel').data('mmenu').close();
                    });
                    
                    if (openSize) {
                        $('#product-quick-view label:contains("Size")').css('color', '#B92F2F');
                    }

                    if (openWishlist) {
                        $('#add-wish-list-btn').click();
                    }
                }
            }
        });

    }

    function AddWishList(productId) {

        var wishListName = $("#wish-list-name").val();
        if (wishListName != undefined && wishListName.trim() !== "") {

            var model = { MemberId: '', WishListName: wishListName };
            $.ajax({
                type: 'POST',
                url: 'https://api.arrowfilms.com/Umbraco/api/' + 'ProductSearch/AddWishList/',
                dataType: 'json',
                data: model,
                cache: false,
                success: function (data) {

                    if (data.data.success) {

                        $("#wish-list-items").prepend('<li><a class="product-in-wishlist" >' +
                            wishListName + '</a></li>');
                        AddProductToWishList(data.data.message, null, productId);
                        $("#create-wishlist-btn").show();
                        $("#wishlist-detail").hide();
                        $("#wish-list-name").val("");
                    } else {
                        PSPAlert(data.data.message, '', 'error');
                    }
                }
            });

        } else {
            PSPAlert("Wish list name required.", '', 'error');
        }
    }

    function ShowWishListBox() {
        $('.wishlist-form').on('click', function (event) {
            event.stopPropagation();
        });

        $("#create-wishlist-btn").hide();
        $("#wishlist-detail").show();
    }

    function GetWishList(productId) {
        var model = { userId: '', currentProductId: productId };
        $.ajax({
            type: 'GET',
            url: 'https://api.arrowfilms.com/Umbraco/api/' + 'ProductSearch/GetWishListByUser/',
            dataType: 'json',
            data: model,
            cache: false,
            success: function (data) {
                $("#wish-list-items").empty();
                $.each(data,
                    function (i, item) {
                        var productExistsInList;
                        if (!item.isProductExists) {
                            productExistsInList = ' onclick="AddProductToWishList(' + item.wishListId + ', this, ' + productId + ')"';
                        } else {
                            productExistsInList = 'class="product-in-wishlist"';
                        }

                        $("#wish-list-items").append('<li><a ' + productExistsInList + '>' +
                            item.wishListName +
                            '</a></li>');
                    });

                $("#wish-list-items").append('<li class="wishlist-form" >' +
                    '<a onclick="ShowWishListBox()" id="create-wishlist-btn"><div class="crossIcon"></div> Create WishList</a>' +
                    '<div style="display:none;" id="wishlist-detail" class="input-group"><input type="text" placeholder="Enter your wishlist name" class="wish-list-text-box" id="wish-list-name"><span class="input-group-btn" onclick="AddWishList(' + productId + ')"><span class="btn white-btn"><div class="crossIcon"></div></span></span></div>' +
                    '</li>');
            }
        });
    }

    function AddProductToCart(productId, productName, productCode, price, category) {
        AddToCart(productId, $("#basket-quantity.product-quick-view-quantity").val(), productName, productCode, price, category);
    }

    function AddProductToWishList(wishListId, elem, productId) {
        AddToWishList('https://api.arrowfilms.com/Umbraco/api/', wishListId, productId);
        if (elem != null) {
            $(elem).addClass("product-in-wishlist");
            $(elem).removeAttr("onclick");
        }
    }

    function QuickViewChangeSize(elem) {
        var selectedProductId = $(elem).val();

        var model = { productId: selectedProductId };
        $.ajax({
            type: 'GET',
            url: 'https://api.arrowfilms.com/Umbraco/api/' + 'ProductSearch/GetProductUrl/',
            dataType: 'json',
            data: model,
            cache: false,
            success: function (data) {
                if (data != undefined && data !== "") {
                    quickViewFunction(null, data.data.productCode);
                }
            }
        });
    }

</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
        $(window).on('load', function () {
            GetShoppingBasket();
        });

        function GetShoppingBasket() {
            EmptyItems();
            $.getJSON("/umbraco/Surface/HeaderSurface/GetShoppingBasket",
                function (json) {
                    if (json.BasketCount === 0) {
                        $('.basketButton').hide();
                        $(".basketDropdown").removeClass('open');
                    } else {
                        $(".basketButton").show();
                        $(".basketCount").append(json.BasketCount);

                        $.each(json.BasketItems, function (i) {
                            var item = "<li class='item'>" +
                                "<div id='itemImage'>" +
                                "<img src='" + 'https://images.arrowfilms.com' + json.BasketItems[i].ProductImage + "' alt='" + json.BasketItems[i].ProductName + "' />" +
                                "</div>" +
                                " <a id='itemName' href='/product-detail/" + json.BasketItems[i].ProductName.replace(/[^\w]/gi, '-').toLowerCase() + "/" + json.BasketItems[i].ProductCode +
                                "'>" + json.BasketItems[i].ProductName + "</a>" +
                                "<span id='itemPrice'>&pound;" + (json.BasketItems[i].Price * json.BasketItems[i].Quantity).toFixed(2) + "</span>" +
                                "<a id='removeItem' onclick='DeleteBasketItem(" + json.BasketItems[i].BasketItemId + ")'></a>" +
                                "</li>";
                            $(".basketItems").append(item);
                        });

                        if (json.BasketSavings > 0) {
                            $("#savingsRow.basket-total-row").show();
                            $(".savingsTotal").append('- &pound;' + json.BasketSavings.toFixed(2));
                        }
                        else {
                            $("#savingsRow.basket-total-row").hide();
                        }

                        $(".basketTotal").append('&pound;' + (json.BasketSubtotal - json.BasketSavings).toFixed(2));
                    }
                });
        }

        function EmptyItems() {
            $(".basketCount").empty();
            $(".basketItems").empty();
            $(".basketTotal").empty();
            $(".savingsTotal").empty();
        }

        function DeleteBasketItem(basketItemId) {
            var model = { basketItemId: basketItemId };

            $.ajax({
                type: 'DELETE',
                url: 'https://api.arrowfilms.com/Umbraco/api/' + 'ProductSearch/DeleteBasketItem/',
                dataType: 'json',
                data: model,
                cache: false,
                success: function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        PSPAlert("Item removed successfully", '', 'success');
                    }

                    GetShoppingBasket();
                }
            });
        }
    </script>

        // Newsletter popup validation
        if ($.isFunction($.fn.validate)) {
            $('#NewsletterPopupForm').validate({
                errorElement: 'span',
                errorClass: 'error',
                focusInvalid: false,
                ignore: "",
                rules: {
                    "PostedNewsletterType.NewsletterTypeIds":
                    {
                        required: true
                    },
                    "PostedInterest.InterestIds":
                    {
                        required: true
                    },
                    Name: {
                        maxlength: 100,
                        required: true
                    },
                    EmailAddress: {
                        maxlength: 100,
                        required: true,
                        email: true
                    }
                },
                messages: {
                    "PostedNewsletterType.NewsletterTypeIds":
                    {
                        required: "Newsletter Type cannot be empty."
                    },
                    "PostedInterest.InterestIds":
                    {
                        required: "Interest cannot be empty."
                    },
                    Name: {
                        maxlength: "Name cannot be empty or greater than 100 alphanumeric characters in length.",
                        required: "Name cannot be empty."
                    },
                    EmailAddress: {
                        maxlength: "Email Address cannot be empty or greater than 100 alphanumeric characters in length.",
                        required: "Email Address cannot be empty.",
                        email: "Email Address must be a valid email address."
                    }
                },
                invalidHandler: function (event, validator) {
                },
                errorPlacement: function (error, element) { // render error placement for each input type
                    var container = $(element).closest(".form-group").find(".error-message");
                    error.appendTo(container);
                },
                highlight: function (element) { // highlight error inputs
                    var parent = $(element).closest('.form-group');
                    parent.removeClass('has-success').addClass('invalid');
                },
                unhighlight: function (element) { // revert the change done by highlight
                },
                success: function (label, element) {
                    var parent = $(element).closest('.form-group');
                    parent.removeClass('invalid').addClass('has-success');
                },
                submitHandler: function (form) {
                    NewsletterSignUp();
                    form.submit();
                }
            });
        }
